migra a marketing-growth quando a North-Star de marca for definida e atingida \u2014 OTD-AC-013).\n>\n> **Decis\u00f5es travadas que este SDD respeita (n\u00e3o reabre):** (1) postura H\u00cdBRIDO rosto-real + avatar-backfill declarado (N\u00c3O 100%-avatar \u2014 FM-AC-014); (2) posicionamento GLOBAL ENGLISH-FIRST (1 trilha-fonte EN + auto-dub nativo da plataforma p/ PT-BR como alcance; voz PT clonada + re-lip-sync como premium); (3) avatar = m\u00f3dulo per-user BYOK PURO **Modelo A** no Canvas Studio \u2014 nunca conta-mestra multiplexada (service bureau proibido); (4) reply engine do X = escopo core; (5) idioma + frequ\u00eancia = per-user opt-in; (6) Gabriel AI = inst\u00e2ncia-zero B2B2C; (7) monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o h\u00edbrida mcoCoins 4\u00d7-floor por render via RPC `deduct_mco_coins` + planos; (8) LGPD biom\u00e9trico Art. 11 + EU AI Act Art. 50.\n>\n> **Constraints Harness n\u00e3o-negoci\u00e1veis aplicados neste SDD:** l\u00f3gica em hooks (hook-first; p\u00e1ginas/componentes display-only); SDD lista apenas o **NOME** da vault key BYOK per-user, nunca o valor do secret; billing SEMPRE via RPC `deduct_mco_coins` (nunca client-side); n\u00f3s do Canvas seguem o registry existente (factory + handles + inspector + nodeTypes map + resolveExecutePayload); toda tabela nova nasce com RLS default-deny + trigger `embed-mcorch-node` + isolamento tenant `user_id`(+`project_id`); Edge Function com JWT validation; artefatos biom\u00e9tricos (`avatar_identities`/`voice_profiles`/`avatar_consents`) = RLS own + storage isolado per-tenant + erase RPC (molde `erase_vision_artifacts`).\n\n---\n\n## 1. 1.2 Canonical Pipeline (FRD \u00a71 in-scope)\n\n```\n[avatarIdentity] rosto-lock (Soul ID / LoRA per-user, ladder Tier1/2/3)\n      \u2502 output: input_face_identity        (avatar_identities, RLS own)\n      \u25bc\n   \u250c\u2500 Identity Gate (ArcFace cosine vs canonical embedding; limiar CALIBRADO\n   \u2502  FAR/FRR/EER \u2014 NUNCA hardcode 0.95/0.7-0.8) \u2192 fail-and-regenerate \u2500\u2500\u2510\n   \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n      \u25bc\n[voiceClone] voz clonada EN-\u00e2ncora (PT-BR premium) \u2014 ElevenLabs PVC/IVC / Cartesia Sonic\n      \u2502 output: input_voice_profile        (voice_profiles, voiceprint cifrado Vault)\n      \u2502 guard: code-switching PT\u2194EN intra-frase BLOQUEADO (FM-AC-013)\n      \u25bc\n[avatarScript] roteiro do \"c\u00e9rebro clonado\" \u2014 RAG sobre mcorch_nodes (GraphRAG, N\u00c3O fine-tune)\n      \u2502 output: input_script               (+ Brand Voice DNA quantificado; sentinel pt-BR+EN)\n      \u25bc\n[talkingHead] lip-sync + anima\u00e7\u00e3o \u2014 roteamento por source_type\n      \u2502 footage_real \u2192 OmniHuman 1.5 (re-lip-sync) \u00b7 avatar_ai \u2192 Hedra/Kling\n      \u2502 output: output_avatar_video (MP4 9:16 1080\u00d71920) via canvas-execute (async)\n      \u2502 deduct AVATAR_VIDEO_COST via RPC deduct_mco_coins (at\u00f4mico, server-side)\n      \u25bc\n[avatarEnhance] Magnific Relight/upscale (polish FINAL via ORG-KEY interna)\n      \u2502 DISCIPLINA DE ORDEM (FM-AC-003): identidade ANTES de enhance; nunca high-Creativity por \u00faltimo\n      \u25bc\n\u2192 Identity Gate (re-check p\u00f3s-enhance) \u2192 Vision QA (uncanny descarte, BYOK-free)\n\u2192 C2PA embed (Art. 50(2) machine-readable) + disclosure marker (Art. 50(4))\n\u2192 creative_assets (source_module='avatar-studio') \u2192 Viral Autopilot (cad\u00eancia 30d h\u00edbrida)\n```\n\n### 1.3 Reuse-First Design Principle\n\nO m\u00f3dulo \u00e9 **constru\u00eddo por reuso** (`00c \u00a7F4` build-vs-buy = construir orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o, integrar motores via BYOK). Reusa, sem reescrever: o Canvas node registry (`src/lib/canvas-node-registry.ts`), `canvas-execute` (cost-gate/daily-cap/async), `video_renders` + `finalize_video_render`, `creative_assets` + `register_creative_asset`, `channel_variants`/`reshape-pillar`/`pillar_atoms`, `user_api_keys` Vault (encrypt trigger + masked/decrypted views \u2014 recipe `20260622210000`), `_shared/sentinel.ts` + `_shared/llm-fallback.ts`, e o pattern de erasure do Vision MCP (`erase_vision_artifacts` + `retention-sweep`). NET-NEW concentra-se em: pipeline de voz, infraestrutura biom\u00e9trica (tabelas/storage/erase), gates de compliance (consent/identity-verification/disclosure/C2PA), reply engine do X, e a jun\u00e7\u00e3o com o Viral Autopilot.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Component Catalog\n\n### 2.1 New Custom Hooks (`src/hooks/`)\n\n| Hook | File | Responsibility | TanStack Query keys |\n|------|------|---------------|---------------------|\n| `useAvatarProviders` | `useAvatarProviders.ts` | L\u00ea estado mascarado dos BYOK providers; invoca `test-api-key` server-side; connect (`.insert`, nunca `.upsert`) / disconnect (RPC); registra os 2 atestados (FR-AC-003/005) | `['avatar-providers', userId]` |\n| `useAvatarConsent` | `useAvatarConsent.ts` | Estado do wizard (3 checkboxes separados); grava `avatar_consents`; aciona identity-verification gate; exp\u00f5e `persona_type` (FR-AC-030/032) | `['avatar-consents', userId]` |\n| `useAvatarRender` | `useAvatarRender.ts` | Orquestra `generate-voice` + `canvas-execute(talkingHead)`; l\u00ea `mco_estimate` (badge); poll de `video_renders` status async (FR-AC-019/020/021) | `['avatar-render', renderId]` |\n| `useAvatarErasure` | `useAvatarErasure.ts` | Dispara `erase-avatar-artifacts` on-revoke; exp\u00f5e atestado de elimina\u00e7\u00e3o (FR-AC-027) | `['avatar-erasure', userId]` |\n| `useAvatarCadence` | `useAvatarCadence.ts` | Opt-in idioma/frequ\u00eancia; dispara ciclo 30d sobre Viral Autopilot; bloqueia config 100%-avatar (FR-AC-033/049/052) | `['avatar-cadence', userId]` |\n\n> **Constraint:** complexidade ciclom\u00e1tica \u2264 10 por hook (NFR-AC-009). Nenhum data-fetch/mutation fora de hooks (NFR-AC-010).\n\n### 2.2 New Display Components (`src/components/`)\n\n| Component | File | Props | Hook consumed |\n|-----------|------|-------|--------------|\n| `AvatarStudioCanvas` | `avatar/AvatarStudioCanvas.tsx` | `{ projectId }` | `useAvatarRender` + `useAvatarStudioStore` |\n| `AvatarIdentityNode` | `avatar/nodes/AvatarIdentityNode.tsx` | `NodeProps` (tier badge, Soul ID/LoRA) | via store / inspector |\n| `VoiceCloneNode` | `avatar/nodes/VoiceCloneNode.tsx` | `NodeProps` (badge \"code-switching-safe\") | via store |\n| `AvatarScriptNode` | `avatar/nodes/AvatarScriptNode.tsx` | `NodeProps` (badge Brand Voice DNA) | via store |\n| `TalkingHeadNode` | `avatar/nodes/TalkingHeadNode.tsx` | `NodeProps` (mco cost badge + status pulse) | `useAvatarRender` |\n| `AvatarEnhanceNode` | `avatar/nodes/AvatarEnhanceNode.tsx` | `NodeProps` (badge \"enhance \u2260 identidade\") | via store |\n| `ConsentWizard` | `avatar/ConsentWizard.tsx` | `{ onComplete }` | `useAvatarConsent` |\n| `IdentityVerificationGate` | `avatar/IdentityVerificationGate.tsx` | `{ personaType }` | `useAvatarConsent` |\n| `AvatarProvidersCard` | `settings/AvatarProvidersCard.tsx` | `{}` | `useAvatarProviders` |\n| `DisclosureMarkerBadge` | `avatar/DisclosureMarkerBadge.tsx` | `{ removable: false }` | \u2014 (display-only, n\u00e3o-remov\u00edvel) |\n| `McoCostBadge` | `avatar/McoCostBadge.tsx` | `{ mcoEstimate }` | \u2014 |\n\n> Todos display-only: chamam hooks e renderizam. Texto de UI / toasts / valida\u00e7\u00f5es em **Portugu\u00eas (Brasil)**; c\u00f3digo/coment\u00e1rios em English.\n\n### 2.3 New / Extended Edge Functions (`supabase/functions/`)\n\n| Function | Path | Status | Auth | mcoCoins | Vault key names (BYOK per-user \u2014 s\u00f3 NOMES) |\n|----------|------|--------|------|----------|----------------|\n| `generate-voice` | `generate-voice/index.ts` | NET-NEW | JWT (user) | **36** (voz EN ~900 chars, recalibr\u00e1vel; Flash/Turbo halva) | `elevenlabs_api_key`, `cartesia_api_key` |\n| `canvas-execute` (kind=`talkingHead`) | `canvas-execute/index.ts` | EXTEND | JWT (user) | **480 Hedra / 2169 OmniHuman / 36 footage-real** (`AVATAR_VIDEO_COST`) | `hedra_api_key`, `omnihuman_api_key`(\u2261`fal_api_key`), `higgsfield_api_key` |\n| `avatar-identity-train` | `avatar-identity-train/index.ts` | NET-NEW | JWT (user) | n/a (config; regenera\u00e7\u00e3o herda custo) | `higgsfield_api_key` |\n| `avatar-script` | `avatar-script/index.ts` | NET-NEW | JWT (user) | **~2** (mapeado a `CONTENT_COST`) | \u2014 (usa per-user LLM key via `_shared/llm-fallback.ts`) |\n| `erase-avatar-artifacts` | `erase-avatar-artifacts/index.ts` | NET-NEW | JWT (user) tenant-guarded | n/a | \u2014 (chama delete nas APIs terceiras com a key per-user em escopo) |\n| `avatar-reply-engine` | `avatar-reply-engine/index.ts` | NET-NEW | service-role (cron) + per-user OAuth | reply texto = `CONTENT_COST`; reply-com-v\u00eddeo = `AVATAR_VIDEO_COST`; cap X separado | `social_credentials.access_token` (per-user OAuth) |\n| `avatar-retention-sweep` | `avatar-retention-sweep/index.ts` | NET-NEW (cron) | service-role | n/a | \u2014 |\n| `orchestrate-step` (step=`avatar_generation`) | `orchestrate-step/index.ts` | EXTEND | service-role | prepaid (suprime self-bill \u2014 molde OTD-VA-010) | \u2014 |\n| `test-api-key` | `test-api-key/index.ts` | EXTEND | JWT (user) | n/a | (testa as novas keys server-side) |\n\n> **JWT stub (toda edge fn user-facing) \u2014 ver \u00a75.3.** `avatar-reply-engine` e `avatar-retention-sweep` s\u00e3o cron/service-role: provam `Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY` in-function + resolvem `user_id` server-trusted da linha (nunca do body \u2014 molde OTD-VA-008).\n\n### 2.4 Zustand Store Slice\n\n| Slice | Store file | State shape | Actions |\n|-------|------------|-------------|---------|\n| Avatar Studio canvas | `src/store/useAvatarStudioStore.ts` | `{ nodes: AvatarNode[], edges, runState: 'idle'\\|'running'\\|'done'\\|'error', activeRenderId, mcoEstimate }` | `addNode`, `updateNodeStatus`, `setRunState`, `syncFromReactFlow` |\n\n> **Hybrid canvas state (molde `useOrchestrationStore`):** ReactFlow owns positions/drag \u2192 debounced 600ms \u2192 persisted (`vm_canvas_projects.graph` analog). Zustand owns run state \u2192 status sync via `useEffect`. Selectors via `useShallow`; `getState()` dentro de intervalos (zero deps) p/ evitar React 18 cascade (Error #185).\n\n### 2.5 New DB Objects\n\n| Object | Type | Purpose | Migration file |\n|--------|------|---------|---------------|\n| `avatar_identities` | Table | LoRA weights ref + ArcFace canonical embedding + `persona_type` + `verification_passed` (RLS own; storage isolado) | `20260701120000_avatar_clone_ai_biometric.sql` |\n| `voice_profiles` | Table | Voiceprint Vault ref + provider + language (RLS own) | `20260701120000_\u2026biometric.sql` |\n| `avatar_consents` | Table (imut\u00e1vel) | Consent por finalidade (LGPD Art. 11) \u2014 vers\u00e3o termo/timestamp/finalidade/IP/user | `20260701120000_\u2026biometric.sql` |\n| `user_api_keys` (+cols) | View/Table ALTER | `hedra_api_key`/`omnihuman_api_key`/`elevenlabs_api_key`/`cartesia_api_key` cifradas | `20260701120500_avatar_byok_keys.sql` |\n| `video_renders` (+cols) | Table ALTER | `composition.avatar` jsonb + `engine IN (\u2026,'hedra','omnihuman','kling')` | `20260701121000_avatar_video_renders.sql` |\n| `erase_avatar_artifacts` | RPC (SECURITY DEFINER) | Cascade tenant-guarded apaga SQL+Storage + dispara delete terceiros (Art. 18) | `20260701121500_erase_avatar_artifacts.sql` |\n| `finalize_avatar_render` | RPC (service-role-only) | Terminal + refund at\u00f4mico (molde `finalize_video_render`) | `20260701121000_avatar_video_renders.sql` |\n| `autopilot_plans` (CHECK) | Table ALTER | Relaxar `platforms` CHECK p/ +youtube/tiktok/instagram/pinterest | `20260701122000_avatar_autopilot_platforms.sql` |\n| `<table>_embedding_idx` | HNSW index | Cosine similarity sobre `avatar_identities.embedding` (mesh) | `20260701120000_\u2026biometric.sql` |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Data Flow Diagrams\n\n### Use Case A: BYOK fail-closed render de talking-head (FR-AC-004/020/021)\n\n```\nUser            useAvatarRender      canvas-execute        DB / RPC            Hedra (BYOK)\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502                   \u2502                    \u2502\n \u2502\u2500[Render 60s]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                    \u2502                   \u2502                    \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502\u2500[POST JWT,kind]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                   \u2502                    \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[getUser(JWT)]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 auth.getUser       \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u25c0\u2500[user ok]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502                    \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[resolve key]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 decrypted_user_api \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502                   \u2502 _keys auth.uid()   \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u25c0\u2500[hedra_api_key?]\u2500\u2502                    \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502  (null \u2192 HTTP 402 hedra_not_configured, ZERO deduct)\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[consent active?]\u25b6\u2502 avatar_consents    \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502  (revoked \u2192 HTTP 403 fail-closed)      \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[hybrid posture]\u2500\u2500\u2502 \u22651 footage real?   \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502  (100%-avatar \u2192 HTTP 403 FM-AC-014)    \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[deduct_mco_coins(user, AVATAR_VIDEO_COST)]\u25b6 RPC (atomic)\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502  (insufficient \u2192 HTTP 402, no media)   \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[render req]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u25c0\u2500[operation_id / async]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[insert video_renders queued]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502    \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502\u25c0\u2500[200 {render_id, status:queued, transport:poll://}]   \u2502    \u2502\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500[badge \"rendering\" + mco debited]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502                    \u2502                    \u2502\n \u2502   (worker claims render \u2192 finalize_avatar_render \u2192 bucket video-studio-assets) \u2502\n```\n\n### Use Case B: Consent revoke \u2192 erasure cascade (FR-AC-027/031, Art. 18)\n\n```\nTitular         useAvatarErasure     erase-avatar-artifacts    DB/Storage         3rd-party engines\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502                        \u2502                   \u2502\n \u2502\u2500[Revoke consent]\u25b6\u2502                    \u2502                        \u2502                   \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502\u2500[POST JWT]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                        \u2502                   \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[tenant guard auth.uid()]\u25b6 avatar_consents.revoked_at=now()\n \u2502                  \u2502                    \u2502\u2500[erase_avatar_artifacts RPC]\u25b6 DELETE avatar_identities/voice_profiles\n \u2502 weights/voice]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 (best-effort + reconcile)\n \u2502                  \u2502                   \u2502\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500[atestado de elimina\u00e7\u00e3o]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502  (pr\u00f3ximo render fail-closed: consent revoked)\n```\n\n### Use Case C: Avatar cadence junction com Viral Autopilot (FR-AC-046..049)\n\n```\nautopilot-cadence-cron \u2192 begin_autopilot_cycle (cap+pr\u00e9-d\u00e9bito at\u00f4mico, prepaid=true)\n  \u2192 orchestrate-step (step=avatar_generation, independente de article_generation)\n      \u2192 AVATAR_FLOW_PLATFORMS gate (youtube/tiktok/instagram/linkedin/x \u2014 n\u00e3o s\u00f3 wordpress)\n      \u2192 enqueue video_renders (engine por source_type) \u2014 prepaid suprime self-bill\n      \u2192 render \u2192 finalize_avatar_render \u2192 reshape-pillar (scope=video) \u2192 channel_variants\n      \u2192 publish gate (C2PA + disclosure n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1vel) \u2192 auto-publish \u2192 publish-social\n  \u2192 finalize_autopilot_cycle (refund cr\u00e9dito-positivo idempotente)\n  \u2192 refund_autopilot_video_enqueue (se !enqueued \u2014 molde 20260624140000)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. API Contract Definitions\n\n> Toda Edge Function valida JWT **antes** de qualquer l\u00f3gica de neg\u00f3cio (\u00a75.3). Ponto de `deduct_mco_coins` anotado por fun\u00e7\u00e3o. mcoCoins SEMPRE server-side via RPC \u2014 nunca client-side (NFR-AC-019).\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/generate-voice` (FR-AC-009)\n\n```typescript\n// Request (Bearer JWT user scope)\ninterface GenerateVoiceRequest {\n  script: string                       // input_script\n  language: 'en' | 'pt-BR'             // idioma \u00daNICO \u2014 nunca code-switch (FR-AC-010)\n  provider: 'elevenlabs' | 'cartesia'\n  voice_profile_id: string             // voice_profiles ref (owner-scoped)\n  project_id?: string\n}\n\n// Response (HTTP 200)\ninterface GenerateVoiceResponse {\n  audio_url: string                    // signed URL curta (bucket privado)\n  creative_asset_id: string            // creative_assets (source_module='avatar-studio')\n  mco_charged: number                  // ~36 (recalibr\u00e1vel)\n}\n\n// mcoCoins deduction point:\n// \u2192 after JWT + BYOK resolve + consent active check:\n//   await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', { p_user_id: user.id, p_amount: VOICE_COST })\n// \u2192 HTTP 402 { error: 'elevenlabs_not_configured', action: '\u2026/dashboard/settings' } (BYOK fail-closed)\n//   OR HTTP 402 saldo insuficiente; HTTP 403 consent revogado / sentinel block; HTTP 422 idioma misto.\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/canvas-execute` (kind=`talkingHead`) (FR-AC-020/021)\n\n```typescript\ninterface TalkingHeadRequest {\n  kind: 'talkingHead'\n  input_face_identity: string          // avatar_identities ref (owner-scoped)\n  input_voice_profile: string          // audio asset ref\n  input_script: string\n  source_type: 'footage_real' | 'avatar_ai'   // dirige roteamento de motor (FR-AC-022)\n  engine?: 'hedra' | 'omnihuman' | 'kling'\n  reference_image_urls?: string[]\n  seed?: number\n}\n\ninterface TalkingHeadResponse {        // HTTP 200 / async\n  render_id: string                    // video_renders row\n  status: 'queued'\n  transport: 'poll://'                 // async (NFR-AC-004 \u2014 UI nunca bloqueia)\n  mco_estimate: number                 // 480 Hedra / 2169 OmniHuman / 36 footage-real\n}\n\n// mcoCoins deduction point (ORDER MATTERS \u2014 fail-closed gates ANTES do d\u00e9bito):\n//   1. JWT validate  2. BYOK resolve (<engine>_api_key per-user \u2192 402 se null)\n//   3. consent active (\u2192403 revoked)  4. hybrid posture (\u22651 footage real \u2192 403 se 100%-avatar)\n//   5. await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', { p_user_id, p_amount: AVATAR_VIDEO_COST[engine] })\n//      \u2192 402 saldo insuficiente SEM gerar m\u00eddia  6. enqueue video_renders\n//   AVATAR_VIDEO_COST \u00c9 SEPARADO de VIDEO_COST (OTD-AC-006 / NFR-AC-019)\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/erase-avatar-artifacts` (FR-AC-027)\n\n```typescript\ninterface EraseAvatarRequest {\n  avatar_identity_id?: string\n  voice_profile_id?: string\n  reason: 'revoke' | 'retention_sweep'\n}\ninterface EraseAvatarResponse {        // HTTP 200\n  erased: { sql: number; storage: number; third_party: string[] }\n  attestation_log_id: string           // log em infra_health_logs (event='avatar_erase')\n}\n// No mcoCoins. tenant-guarded (403 tenant mismatch). Notifica\u00e7\u00e3o a terceiros (Art. 18) =\n// best-effort com reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o (NFR-AC-008, eventually consistent \u226424h).\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/avatar-identity-train` (FR-AC-012/013/032)\n\n```typescript\ninterface AvatarIdentityTrainRequest {\n  training_photo_urls: string[]        // \u226520 (Soul ID) owner-scoped\n  liveness_selfie_url: string          // identity-verification gate (self-clone)\n  persona_type: 'self_clone' | 'fictional'\n  tier: 1 | 2 | 3\n}\ninterface AvatarIdentityTrainResponse { // HTTP 200 / async\n  avatar_identity_id: string\n  verification_passed: boolean          // s\u00f3 true ap\u00f3s selfie-liveness bater (self-clone)\n  status: 'queued'\n}\n// GATE (ANTES de gerar weights): identity-verification \u2014 selfie-liveness vs training photos.\n//   Falha \u2192 HTTP 403, N\u00c3O persiste artefato biom\u00e9trico (clone de terceiro bloqueado por design).\n//   Requer avatar_consents ativos (train_lora + face_embedding) \u2192 sen\u00e3o HTTP 403.\n// No deduct aqui; render subsequente cobra. Commercial-license gate bloqueia pesos non-commercial.\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/avatar-reply-engine` (FR-AC-042..045)\n\n```typescript\n// cron/service-role driver: prova Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY in-function; user_id server-trusted da linha.\ninterface ReplyEngineCycle {\n  user_id: string                      // server-trusted (NUNCA do body \u2014 OTD-VA-008)\n  poll_window: 'mentions'\n}\n// Loop: GET /2/users/:id/mentions (\u2264300/15min) \u2192 dedup 24h UTC \u2192 gera reply (c\u00e9rebro-RAG + sentinel)\n//   \u2192 POST /2/tweets (\u2264100/15min). Pay-per-use: read $0.005 + reply $0.015 / $0.20-com-link.\n//   Cap de gasto per-user (per-plano + di\u00e1rio, molde deepsearch.run). Disclosure deepfake aplicada.\n//   reply-com-link s\u00f3 quando explicitamente monetizado; reply-com-v\u00eddeo herda AVATAR_VIDEO_COST.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Security Design\n\n### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE por componente)\n\n| Component | Threat | STRIDE Type | Severity | Mitigation |\n|-----------|--------|-------------|----------|------------|\n| `generate-voice` / `canvas-execute` / `avatar-identity-train` | Acesso n\u00e3o-autenticado a s\u00edntese paga | Spoofing | High | JWT validation no entry-point (\u00a75.3); 401 sem token v\u00e1lido |\n| `avatar_identities` / `voice_profiles` | Leitura cross-tenant de PII biom\u00e9trica (weights/voiceprint de outro tenant) | Information Disclosure | **Critical** | RLS own `auth.uid() = user_id` default-deny; FMEA-011; storage prefixo `user_id/` + signed URL curta (NFR-AC-013) |\n| `deduct_mco_coins` (avatar render) | Bypass client-side da dedu\u00e7\u00e3o | Tampering | **Critical** | RPC server-side only; `AVATAR_VIDEO_COST` \u2260 `VIDEO_COST`; zero dedu\u00e7\u00e3o no cliente (NFR-AC-019) |\n| BYOK key resolution | Fan-out de conta-mestra (service bureau) | Elevation of Privilege | **Critical** | Invariante anti-conta-mestra: toda chamada resolve `auth.uid()`; grep audit zero fan-out (FR-AC-006); Modelo A estrito |\n| `avatar_consents` | Falsifica\u00e7\u00e3o/edi\u00e7\u00e3o retroativa de consentimento | Tampering / Repudiation | **Critical** | Tabela imut\u00e1vel (sem UPDATE de conte\u00fado; s\u00f3 `revoked_at`); vers\u00e3o termo + IP + timestamp (LGPD Art. 11) |\n| `avatar-identity-train` | Clone de terceiro privado real (TikTok bane) | Spoofing (identidade) | **Critical** | Identity-verification gate: selfie-liveness vs fotos de treino ANTES de weights; falha \u2192 n\u00e3o persiste (FR-AC-032) |\n| publish gate / `avatar-reply-engine` | Publicar deepfake sem disclosure (EU AI Act / CONAR) | Repudiation / Compliance | **Critical** | Disclosure-by-default n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1vel: C2PA Art.50(2) + deepfake disclosure Art.50(4); render sem disclosure bloqueado (FR-AC-035/036) |\n| `erase-avatar-artifacts` | Erase de artefato de outro tenant | Elevation of Privilege | High | Tenant-guarded `auth.uid()`; 403 tenant mismatch; cascade s\u00f3 na linha do titular |\n| `avatar-script` (RAG) | Prompt injection / jailbreak via mentions ou material RAG | Tampering | High | `_shared/sentinel.ts` (pt-BR + EN fam\u00edlias f1-f7); sanitiza\u00e7\u00e3o de fonte n\u00e3o-confi\u00e1vel |\n| `avatar-reply-engine` | DoS de gasto (gasto X descontrolado) | Denial of Service | High | Cap de gasto per-user (per-plano + di\u00e1rio); rate-limit poll (\u2264300/15min); reply-com-link gated |\n| Storage bucket biom\u00e9trico | Vazamento por URL longa/p\u00fablica | Information Disclosure | High | Bucket privado; signed URL curta owner-scoped; segregado de `mcorch_nodes`/conte\u00fado (FR-AC-026) |\n| `voice_profiles` / Art. 5(1)(g) | Inferir caracter\u00edsticas sens\u00edveis de pessoas dos v\u00eddeos | Information Disclosure | Medium | Red-line por design: uso restrito \u00e0 identidade do pr\u00f3prio titular consentido (FR-AC-029) |\n\n### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions\n\n```sql\n-- avatar_identities (biometric \u2014 RLS own default-deny)\nALTER TABLE avatar_identities ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_identities_select_own\"\n  ON avatar_identities FOR SELECT USING (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_identities_insert_own\"\n  ON avatar_identities FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_identities_update_own\"\n  ON avatar_identities FOR UPDATE USING (user_id = auth.uid()) WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n-- DELETE s\u00f3 via RPC erase_avatar_artifacts (SECURITY DEFINER tenant-guarded) \u2014 sem policy DELETE direta.\n\n-- voice_profiles \u2014 id\u00eantico padr\u00e3o own.\n-- avatar_consents \u2014 SELECT/INSERT own; SEM UPDATE de conte\u00fado (imut\u00e1vel); revoke = UPDATE s\u00f3 de revoked_at.\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_consents_select_own\"\n  ON avatar_consents FOR SELECT USING (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_consents_insert_own\"\n  ON avatar_consents FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_consents_revoke_own\"\n  ON avatar_consents FOR UPDATE USING (user_id = auth.uid())\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());  -- trigger restringe colunas mut\u00e1veis a revoked_at\n```\n\n### 5.3 JWT Validation Points\n\nToda Edge Function user-facing segue o stub (CISSP CBK \u2014 authentication at trust boundary):\n\n```typescript\nconst authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization')\nif (!authHeader) return json({ error: 'unauthorized' }, 401)\nconst { data: { user }, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser(\n  authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '')\n)\nif (error || !user) return json({ error: 'unauthorized' }, 401)\n// \u2192 s\u00f3 depois: BYOK resolve \u2192 consent check \u2192 hybrid posture \u2192 deduct_mco_coins \u2192 business logic\n```\n\nFun\u00e7\u00f5es cron/service-role (`avatar-reply-engine`, `avatar-retention-sweep`, `orchestrate-step`):\n\n```typescript\nconst auth = req.headers.get('Authorization') ?? ''\nif (auth !== `Bearer ${Deno.env.get('SB_SECRET_KEY')}`) return json({ error: 'forbidden' }, 403)\n// user_id resolvido server-trusted da linha (video_renders / autopilot_cycles) \u2014 NUNCA do body (OTD-VA-008)\n```\n\n### 5.4 Secrets Inventory (NOMES de vault key \u2014 nunca valores)\n\n| Secret Name | Tipo | Used In | Resolution | Rotation Policy |\n|-------------|------|---------|------------|----------------|\n| `hedra_api_key` | BYOK per-user (`user_api_keys`, Vault) | `canvas-execute(talkingHead)` | `auth.uid()` decrypted view; 402 se null | Per-user (titular rotaciona) |\n| `omnihuman_api_key` (\u2261 `fal_api_key`) | BYOK per-user (Vault) | `canvas-execute(talkingHead)` re-lip-sync | `auth.uid()`; 402 se null | Per-user |\n| `higgsfield_api_key` | BYOK per-user (Vault \u2014 j\u00e1 existe) | `avatar-identity-train`, Kling render | `auth.uid()`; 402 se null | Per-user |\n| `elevenlabs_api_key` | BYOK per-user (Vault) | `generate-voice` | `auth.uid()`; 402 se null | Per-user |\n| `cartesia_api_key` | BYOK per-user (Vault) | `generate-voice` (real-time Sonic) | `auth.uid()`; 402 se null | Per-user |\n| `MAGNIFIC_ORG_API_KEY` | **ORG-key (system-level, N\u00c3O per-user)** | `avatar-enhance` / `canvas-execute(avatarEnhance)` | env vault (custo interno MCORCH; per-user PROIBIDO sem consent escrito) | Org-managed |\n| `social_credentials.access_token` | per-user OAuth (Vault) | `avatar-reply-engine`, `publish-social` | `auth.uid()` / linha do titular | Per-user OAuth refresh |\n| `SB_SECRET_KEY` | service-role | cron/service-role fns | env | System-managed |\n| `MESH_EMBED_OPENROUTER_KEY` | system (mesh) | `embed-mcorch-node` (trigger biom\u00e9trico) | env | System-managed |\n\n> \u26a0\ufe0f **NUNCA commit de valor de secret.** S\u00f3 NOMES de vault key aparecem na documenta\u00e7\u00e3o. BYOK per-user fail-closed: zero `Deno.env.get('<provider>')` em fluxo user-facing de s\u00edntese (NFR-AC-012). Magnific \u00e9 a \u00fanica exce\u00e7\u00e3o declarada (org-key interna \u2014 `00c \u00a7F1`).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints\n\n| Infrastructure | Usage | Impact |\n|----------------|-------|--------|\n| **Render worker** (`scripts/avatar-render-bridge.ts`, molde `video-bridge.ts` + systemd `avatar-render-bridge.service`) | Claim at\u00f4mico de `video_renders` (engine \u2208 hedra/omnihuman/kling) \u2192 chama API BYOK per-user \u2192 upload bucket `video-studio-assets` (prefixo `user_id/`) \u2192 `finalize_avatar_render` \u2192 kick `reshape-pillar scope=video` | Off-band render async (NFR-AC-004); UI nunca bloqueia; refund at\u00f4mico em fail |\n| **Storage bucket** `video-studio-assets` (+ prefixo biom\u00e9trico isolado) | Artefatos biom\u00e9tricos (LoRA weights, voiceprint refs) em prefixo privado `user_id/`; signed URL curta owner-scoped | Segrega\u00e7\u00e3o per-tenant (FR-AC-026); nenhuma URL p\u00fablica |\n| **Supabase Vault** (`vault.secrets` via `vault_upsert_secret`) | Voiceprints cifrados + BYOK keys per-user (encrypt trigger idempotente, recipe `20260622210000`) | PII biom\u00e9trica nunca em claro;
